import { Body,Controller,Get,Param,Post,Req,UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClinicalAuthenticationGuard } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-authentication.guard';
import { ClinicalRequestContextService } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-request-context.service';
import { EdgeGatewayAuthenticationGuard, type EdgeGatewayPrincipal } from './edge-gateway-auth.guard';
import { EdgeService } from './edge.service';
import { EdgeCommandDto,EdgeEventDto,EdgeHeartbeatDto,EdgeFaultDto,EdgeUploadStartDto,EdgeUploadPartDto,EdgeUploadFinalizeDto,EdgeCommandAckDto } from './edge.dto';

@UseGuards(ClinicalAuthenticationGuard)
@Controller('edge/control')
export class EdgeControlController {
  constructor(private readonly s:EdgeService,private readonly c:ClinicalRequestContextService){}

  @Post('commands')
  command(@Body() d:EdgeCommandDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.queueCommand(d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
}

@UseGuards(EdgeGatewayAuthenticationGuard)
@Controller('edge/gateway')
export class EdgeGatewayController {
  constructor(private readonly s:EdgeService){}

  @Get('commands')
  pending(@Req() r:any){return this.s.pendingForGateway(r.edgeGateway as EdgeGatewayPrincipal);}

  @Post('events')
  event(@Body() d:EdgeEventDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.ingestEventFromGateway(d,r.edgeGateway as EdgeGatewayPrincipal);}

  @Post('heartbeat')
  heartbeat(@Body() d:EdgeHeartbeatDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.heartbeat(d,r.edgeGateway as EdgeGatewayPrincipal);}

  @Post('faults')
  fault(@Body() d:EdgeFaultDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.recordFault(d,r.edgeGateway as EdgeGatewayPrincipal);}

  @Post('uploads')
  upload(@Body() d:EdgeUploadStartDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.startUpload(d,r.edgeGateway as EdgeGatewayPrincipal);}

  @Post('uploads/:uploadId/parts')
  uploadPart(@Param('uploadId') id:string,@Body() d:EdgeUploadPartDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.recordUploadPart(id,d,r.edgeGateway as EdgeGatewayPrincipal);}

  @Post('uploads/:uploadId/finalize')
  uploadFinalize(@Param('uploadId') id:string,@Body() d:EdgeUploadFinalizeDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.finalizeUpload(id,d,r.edgeGateway as EdgeGatewayPrincipal);}


  @Post('command-acks')
  commandAck(@Body() d:EdgeCommandAckDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.commandAck(d,r.edgeGateway as EdgeGatewayPrincipal);}

}

import { IsIn,IsInt,IsObject,IsOptional,IsString,IsUUID,Min } from '../../clinical-common/dto';
export class EdgeCommandDto{
 @IsUUID() gatewayId!:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() acquisitionSessionId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() deviceRuntimeBindingId?:string;
 @IsIn(['SYNC_RUNTIME_CONFIG','PREPARE_ACQUISITION','ARM_DATA_CAPTURE','PAUSE_DATA_INGEST','RESUME_DATA_INGEST','STOP_DATA_INGEST','ABORT_ACQUISITION','REQUEST_DEVICE_HEALTH','RUN_INTERFACE_TEST','REQUEST_OUTBOX_REPLAY']) commandType!:string;
 @IsString() idempotencyKey!:string; @IsObject() payload!:Record<string,unknown>; @IsString() expiresAt!:string;
}
export class EdgeEventDto{
 @IsUUID() gatewayId!:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() connectionSessionId?:string; @IsString() messageId!:string;
 @IsInt() @Min(0) gatewaySequence!:number; @IsString() messageType!:string; @IsString() idempotencyKey!:string;
 @IsString() occurredAt!:string; @IsObject() payload!:Record<string,unknown>; @IsString() payloadSha256!:string;
}
export class EdgeHeartbeatDto {
 @IsInt() @Min(0) sequenceNo!:number; @IsString() observedAt!:string;
 @IsIn(['OK','WARNING','ERROR']) agentStatus!:string;
 @IsOptional() cpuPercent?:number; @IsOptional() memoryPercent?:number; @IsOptional() diskPercent?:number;
 @IsOptional() @IsInt() @Min(0) localQueueDepth?:number; @IsOptional() @IsInt() @Min(0) localCacheBytes?:number;
 @IsOptional() networkRttMs?:number; @IsOptional() @IsInt() clockOffsetMs?:number; @IsOptional() @IsInt() @Min(0) activeDeviceCount?:number;
 @IsOptional() @IsObject() errorSummary?:Record<string,unknown>;
}
export class EdgeFaultDto {
 @IsOptional() @IsUUID() deviceRuntimeBindingId?:string; @IsOptional() @IsUUID() acquisitionSessionId?:string;
 @IsIn(['INFO','WARNING','ERROR','CRITICAL']) severity!:string; @IsString() faultCode!:string; @IsString() subsystem!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsObject() metadata?:Record<string,unknown>; @IsString() observedAt!:string;
}
export class EdgeUploadStartDto {
 @IsUUID() artifactId!:string; @IsString() originIdempotencyKey!:string; @IsInt() @Min(16384) chunkSizeBytes!:number;
 @IsInt() @Min(0) expectedTotalBytes!:number; @IsString() expectedSha256!:string; @IsString() expiresAt!:string;
}
export class EdgeUploadPartDto {
 @IsInt() @Min(1) partNo!:number; @IsInt() @Min(0) offsetBytes!:number; @IsInt() @Min(0) sizeBytes!:number;
 @IsString() expectedPartSha256!:string; @IsString() observedPartSha256!:string;
}
export class EdgeUploadFinalizeDto { @IsString() observedWholeSha256!:string; }
export class EdgeCommandAckDto {
 @IsUUID() commandMessageId!:string;
 @IsIn(['ACCEPTED','REJECTED','COMPLETED','FAILED','DUPLICATE']) ackStatus!:string;
 @IsOptional() @IsString() reasonCode?:string;
 @IsUUID() edgeMessageId!:string; @IsInt() @Min(0) gatewaySequence!:number; @IsString() occurredAt!:string;
 @IsObject() payload!:Record<string,unknown>;
}

import { Controller,Get,Param,Query,Req,UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClinicalAuthenticationGuard } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-authentication.guard';
import { ReadinessService } from './readiness.service';
import { ClinicalRequestContextService } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-request-context.service';

@UseGuards(ClinicalAuthenticationGuard)
@Controller('clinical/readiness')
export class ReadinessController {
 constructor(private readonly s:ReadinessService,private readonly c:ClinicalRequestContextService){}
 @Get('sites/:siteId/devices') devices(@Param('siteId') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.siteInventory(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Get('sites/:siteId/devices/operational') operational(@Param('siteId') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.siteOperationalDevices(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Get('sites/:siteId/exams') exams(@Param('siteId') id:string,@Query('examDefinitionId') exam:string|undefined,@Req() r:any){return this.s.examReadiness(id,exam,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Get('sites/:siteId/exams/:examDefinitionId/devices') candidates(@Param('siteId') site:string,@Param('examDefinitionId') exam:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.candidateDevices(site,exam,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Get('devices/:deviceInstanceId/adapter-eligibility') adapter(@Param('deviceInstanceId') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.adapterEligibility(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Get('sites/:siteId/workforce-capabilities') workforce(@Param('siteId') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.currentWorkforce(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
}

PATH: C:\realtime-server\src\devices\readiness\readiness.service.ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClinicalPgService } from '../../clinical-database/clinical-pg.service';
import { ClinicalReadExecutor } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-read.executor';
import type { ClinicalRequestContext } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-types';

@Injectable()
export class ReadinessService {
  constructor(private readonly db:ClinicalPgService,private readonly reads:ClinicalReadExecutor){}

  siteInventory(siteId:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.reads.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_READINESS_READ',action:'READINESS_SITE_INVENTORY',resourceType:'READINESS',resourceId:siteId,handler:client=>client.query(`SELECT i.* FROM v_site_device_inventory i JOIN medical_device_instances d ON d.id=i.device_instance_id WHERE d.site_id=$1 ORDER BY i.device_type_code,i.asset_tag`,[siteId]).then(r=>r.rows)});}

  siteOperationalDevices(siteId:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.reads.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_READINESS_READ',action:'READINESS_OPERATIONAL_DEVICES',resourceType:'READINESS',resourceId:siteId,handler:client=>client.query(`SELECT * FROM v_medical_device_operational_availability WHERE site_id=$1 ORDER BY device_type_code,asset_tag`,[siteId]).then(r=>r.rows)});}

  examReadiness(siteId:string,examDefinitionId:string|undefined,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.reads.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_READINESS_READ',action:'READINESS_EXAMS',resourceType:'READINESS',resourceId:siteId,handler:async(client)=>{const values:any[]=[siteId];let extra='';if(examDefinitionId){values.push(examDefinitionId);extra=` AND exam_definition_id=$2`;}return (await client.query(`SELECT * FROM v_site_exam_current_readiness WHERE site_id=$1${extra} ORDER BY exam_code`,values)).rows;}});}

  candidateDevices(siteId:string,examDefinitionId:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.reads.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_READINESS_READ',action:'READINESS_CANDIDATE_DEVICES',resourceType:'READINESS',resourceId:siteId,handler:client=>client.query(`SELECT * FROM v_site_exam_candidate_devices WHERE site_id=$1 AND exam_definition_id=$2 ORDER BY device_instance_id`,[siteId,examDefinitionId]).then(r=>r.rows)});}

  adapterEligibility(deviceInstanceId:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.reads.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_READINESS_READ',action:'READINESS_ADAPTER_ELIGIBILITY',resourceType:'READINESS',resourceId:deviceInstanceId,handler:client=>client.query(`SELECT * FROM v_edge_runtime_clinical_adapter_eligibility WHERE device_instance_id=$1`,[deviceInstanceId]).then(r=>r.rows)});}

  currentWorkforce(siteId:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.reads.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_READINESS_READ',action:'READINESS_WORKFORCE',resourceType:'READINESS',resourceId:siteId,handler:client=>client.query(`SELECT * FROM v_current_site_staff_capabilities WHERE site_id=$1 ORDER BY workforce_id,workforce_capability_code`,[siteId]).then(r=>r.rows)});}

}

import { Body,Controller,Get,Param,Post,Req,UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ClinicalAuthenticationGuard } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-authentication.guard';
import { ClinicalRequestContextService } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-request-context.service';
import { AcquisitionService } from './acquisition.service';
import { AcquisitionTransitionDto,ArtifactIntegrityDto,BindDeviceDto,BindWorkforceDto,CreateAcquisitionDto,CreateQualityReviewDto,CreateRepeatDto,QualityOutcomeDto,RegisterArtifactDto,CreateTransferDto,TransferProgressDto,TransferStateDto,RepeatAcceptDto,RepeatTransitionDto,CompleteExamOrderDto } from './acquisition.dto';
@UseGuards(ClinicalAuthenticationGuard) @Controller('clinical')
export class AcquisitionController{
 constructor(private readonly s:AcquisitionService,private readonly c:ClinicalRequestContextService){}
 @Post('acquisitions') create(@Body() d:CreateAcquisitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.create(d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Get('acquisitions/:id') get(@Param('id') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.get(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/resources/workforce') bw(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:BindWorkforceDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.bindWorkforce(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/resources/device') bd(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:BindDeviceDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.bindDevice(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/resources/commit') rb(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AcquisitionTransitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.resourcesBound(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/ready') ready(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AcquisitionTransitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.ready(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/start') start(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AcquisitionTransitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.start(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/pause') pause(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AcquisitionTransitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.pause(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/resume') resume(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AcquisitionTransitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.resume(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/stop') stop(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AcquisitionTransitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.stop(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/data-received') dr(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AcquisitionTransitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.dataReceived(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/quality-pending') qp(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:AcquisitionTransitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.qualityPending(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/artifacts') artifact(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:RegisterArtifactDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.registerArtifact(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('artifacts/:id/received') received(@Param('id') id:string,@Req() r:any){return this.s.markArtifactReceived(id,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('artifacts/:id/integrity') integrity(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:ArtifactIntegrityDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.verifyArtifact(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/quality-review') q(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CreateQualityReviewDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.createQualityReview(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('quality-reviews/:id/finalize') qf(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:QualityOutcomeDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.finalizeQuality(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('acquisitions/:id/repeat') repeat(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CreateRepeatDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.createRepeat(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('artifacts/:id/transfers') transfer(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CreateTransferDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.createTransfer(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('artifact-transfers/:id/progress') transferProgress(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:TransferProgressDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.transferProgress(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('artifact-transfers/:id/state') transferState(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:TransferStateDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.transferState(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('repeat-requests/:id/accept') repeatAccept(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:RepeatAcceptDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.acceptRepeat(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('repeat-requests/:id/satisfy') repeatSatisfy(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:RepeatTransitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.satisfyRepeat(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('repeat-requests/:id/decline') repeatDecline(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:RepeatTransitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.declineRepeat(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('repeat-requests/:id/cancel') repeatCancel(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:RepeatTransitionDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.cancelRepeat(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}
 @Post('exam-orders/:id/complete-acquisition') completeOrder(@Param('id') id:string,@Body() d:CompleteExamOrderDto,@Req() r:any){return this.s.completeExamOrder(id,d,this.c.fromRequest(r));}

}

PATH: C:\realtime-server\src\clinical\acquisition\acquisition.service.ts
import { ConflictException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { ClinicalRequestContext } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-types';
import { ClinicalTableRepository } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-table.repository';
import { ClinicalCommandExecutor } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-command.executor';
import { ClinicalReadExecutor } from '../../clinical-common/clinical-read.executor';

@Injectable()
export class AcquisitionService {
 constructor(private readonly tables:ClinicalTableRepository,private readonly commands:ClinicalCommandExecutor,private readonly reads:ClinicalReadExecutor){}
 create(dto:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_ACQUISITION_OPERATE',action:'ACQUISITION_CREATE',resourceType:'ACQUISITION',
   handler:client=>this.tables.insert('exam_acquisition_sessions',{
    acquisition_number:`ACQ-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID().slice(0,8)}`,exam_order_id:dto.examOrderId,attempt_no:dto.attemptNo,
    repeat_of_acquisition_id:dto.repeatOfAcquisitionId,site_id:dto.siteId,room_id:dto.roomId,
    planned_start_at:dto.plannedStartAt?new Date(dto.plannedStartAt):undefined,created_by_user_id:ctx.userId,
   },client)});
 }
 get(id:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.reads.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_ACQUISITION_OPERATE',action:'ACQUISITION_READ',resourceType:'ACQUISITION',resourceId:id,handler:client=>this.tables.findById('exam_acquisition_sessions',id,client)});}
 bindWorkforce(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_ACQUISITION_OPERATE',action:'ACQUISITION_BIND_WORKFORCE',resourceType:'ACQUISITION',resourceId:id,
   handler:client=>this.tables.insert('acquisition_workforce_bindings',{
    acquisition_session_id:id,workforce_id:d.workforceId,requirement_option_id:d.requirementOptionId,
    staff_capability_grant_id:d.staffCapabilityGrantId,staff_device_qualification_id:d.staffDeviceQualificationId,
    binding_role:d.bindingRole,binding_status:'RESERVED',exclusive_binding:d.exclusiveBinding??false,
    reserved_at:new Date(),lease_expires_at:new Date(d.leaseExpiresAt),created_by_user_id:ctx.userId,
   },client)});
 }
 bindDevice(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_ACQUISITION_OPERATE',action:'ACQUISITION_BIND_DEVICE',resourceType:'ACQUISITION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const availability=await client.query<any>(`
      SELECT technical_availability,primary_reason_code
      FROM v_medical_device_operational_availability
      WHERE device_instance_id=$1
    `,[d.deviceInstanceId]);
    const tech=availability.rows[0];
    if(!tech) throw new ConflictException('Device operational availability is unknown');
    if(tech.technical_availability==='UNAVAILABLE') throw new ConflictException(`Device unavailable: ${tech.primary_reason_code}`);
    const iface=await client.query<any>(`
      SELECT interface_availability,device_instance_id
      FROM v_device_interface_current_availability
      WHERE device_interface_id=$1
    `,[d.deviceInterfaceId]);
    const intf=iface.rows[0];
    if(!intf || intf.device_instance_id!==d.deviceInstanceId) throw new ConflictException('Device interface mismatch');
    if(!['AVAILABLE','DEGRADED'].includes(intf.interface_availability)) throw new ConflictException(`Device interface unavailable: ${intf.interface_availability}`);
    return this.tables.insert('acquisition_device_bindings',{
      acquisition_session_id:id,device_instance_id:d.deviceInstanceId,device_interface_id:d.deviceInterfaceId,binding_purpose:d.bindingPurpose,
      binding_status:'RESERVED',exclusive_binding:d.exclusiveBinding??true,required_in_acquisition_room:d.requiredInAcquisitionRoom??true,
      reserved_at:new Date(),lease_expires_at:new Date(d.leaseExpiresAt),
      technical_availability_at_binding:tech.technical_availability,technical_reason_at_binding:tech.primary_reason_code,
      interface_availability_at_binding:intf.interface_availability,created_by_user_id:ctx.userId,
    },client);
   }});
 }
 private transition(id:string,to:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext,reasonOverrides:any[]=[]){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_ACQUISITION_OPERATE',action:`ACQUISITION_${to}`,resourceType:'ACQUISITION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const args=[id,to,d.expectedVersion,ctx.userId,ctx.workforceId??null,d.reasonCode,d.reasonRef??null,ctx.requestId,
      d.allowConditional??false,d.conditionalJustificationRef??null,d.conditionalAuthorizationGrantId??null,...reasonOverrides];
    await this.tables.call('transition_acquisition',args,client);
    return this.tables.findById('exam_acquisition_sessions',id,client);
   }});
 }
 resourcesBound(id:string,d:any,c:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.transition(id,'RESOURCES_BOUND',d,c);}
 ready(id:string,d:any,c:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.transition(id,'READY',d,c);}
 start(id:string,d:any,c:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.transition(id,'ACQUIRING',d,c);}
 pause(id:string,d:any,c:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.transition(id,'PAUSED',d,c);}
 resume(id:string,d:any,c:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.transition(id,'ACQUIRING',d,c);}
 stop(id:string,d:any,c:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.transition(id,'TRANSFER_PENDING',d,c);}
 dataReceived(id:string,d:any,c:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.transition(id,'DATA_RECEIVED',d,c);}
 qualityPending(id:string,d:any,c:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.transition(id,'QUALITY_REVIEW_PENDING',d,c);}
 abort(id:string,d:any,c:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.transition(id,'ABORTED',d,c,[d.reasonCode]);}
 fail(id:string,d:any,c:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.transition(id,'FAILED',d,c,[null,d.reasonCode]);}
 cancel(id:string,d:any,c:ClinicalRequestContext){return this.transition(id,'CANCELLED',d,c,[null,null,d.reasonCode]);}

 registerArtifact(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_ARTIFACT_REGISTER',action:'ARTIFACT_REGISTER',resourceType:'ACQUISITION',resourceId:id,
   handler:client=>this.tables.insert('clinical_artifacts',{
    acquisition_session_id:id,origin_idempotency_key:d.originIdempotencyKey,artifact_kind:d.artifactKind,source_mode:d.sourceMode,
    source_device_instance_id:d.sourceDeviceInstanceId,source_device_interface_id:d.sourceDeviceInterfaceId,source_gateway_id:d.sourceGatewayId,
    captured_by_workforce_id:d.capturedByWorkforceId,manually_verified_by_workforce_id:d.manuallyVerifiedByWorkforceId,
    storage_backend:d.storageBackend,storage_ref:d.storageRef,content_type:d.contentType,format_standard:d.formatStandard,size_bytes:d.sizeBytes,
    freshness_class:d.freshnessClass,device_captured_at:d.deviceCapturedAt?new Date(d.deviceCapturedAt):undefined,
    derived_from_artifact_id:d.derivedFromArtifactId,processing_version:d.processingVersion,
   },client)});
 }

      review_status:'DRAFT',outcome:d.outcome,review_started_at:new Date(),limitation_or_instruction_ref:d.limitationOrInstructionRef,correction_of_review_id:d.correctionOfReviewId,
    },client);
   }});
 }

   }});
 }
 createRepeat(id:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_QUALITY_REVIEW',action:'REPEAT_REQUEST_CREATE',resourceType:'ACQUISITION',resourceId:id,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    if(!ctx.workforceId) throw new ConflictException('Workforce identity required');
    const acq:any=await this.tables.findById('exam_acquisition_sessions',id,client);
    return this.tables.insert('exam_repeat_requests',{
      exam_order_id:acq.exam_order_id,source_acquisition_id:id,requested_by_workforce_id:ctx.workforceId,
      quality_review_id:d.qualityReviewId,reason_code:d.reasonCode,instruction_ref:d.instructionRef,repeat_status:'OPEN',
    },client);
   }});
 }
 transitionRepeat(repeatId:string,to:string,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext,extra:{reasonCode:string,newAcquisitionId?:string,capabilityGrantId?:string,declineReasonCode?:string}){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_ACQUISITION_OPERATE',action:`REPEAT_${to}`,resourceType:'REPEAT_REQUEST',resourceId:repeatId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    await this.tables.call('transition_repeat_request',[repeatId,to,ctx.userId,ctx.workforceId??null,extra.reasonCode,ctx.requestId,
      extra.newAcquisitionId??null,extra.capabilityGrantId??null,extra.declineReasonCode??null],client);
    return this.tables.findById('exam_repeat_requests',repeatId,client);
   }});
 }
 createTransfer(artifactId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_ARTIFACT_REGISTER',action:'ARTIFACT_TRANSFER_CREATE',resourceType:'CLINICAL_ARTIFACT',resourceId:artifactId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const transfer:any=await this.tables.insert('artifact_transfer_attempts',{
      artifact_id:artifactId,attempt_no:d.attemptNo,source_gateway_id:d.sourceGatewayId,transfer_mode:d.transferMode,
      transport_protocol:d.transportProtocol,expected_bytes:d.expectedBytes,resume_from_byte:d.resumeFromByte,expected_sha256:d.expectedSha256,
    },client);
    await this.tables.insert('artifact_transfer_events',{
      transfer_attempt_id:transfer.id,event_type:'STARTED',bytes_received:0,source_offset:d.resumeFromByte??0,request_id:ctx.requestId,
    },client);
    return transfer;
   }});
 }

 transferProgress(transferId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_ARTIFACT_REGISTER',action:'ARTIFACT_TRANSFER_PROGRESS',resourceType:'CLINICAL_ARTIFACT',resourceId:transferId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const tr:any=await this.tables.findById('artifact_transfer_attempts',transferId,client,true);
    if(!['INITIATED','IN_PROGRESS','PAUSED'].includes(tr.transfer_status)) throw new ConflictException('Transfer is terminal');
    if(Number(d.receivedBytes)<Number(tr.received_bytes)) throw new ConflictException('Transfer byte count cannot move backwards');
    const status=tr.transfer_status==='INITIATED'?'IN_PROGRESS':tr.transfer_status;
    const updated=await this.tables.updateById('artifact_transfer_attempts',transferId,{
      transfer_status:status,received_bytes:d.receivedBytes,last_progress_at:new Date(),
    },client);
    await this.tables.insert('artifact_transfer_events',{
      transfer_attempt_id:transferId,event_type:'PROGRESS',bytes_received:d.receivedBytes,source_offset:d.sourceOffset,request_id:ctx.requestId,
    },client);
    return updated;
   }});
 }

 transferState(transferId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_ARTIFACT_REGISTER',action:`ARTIFACT_TRANSFER_${d.status}`,resourceType:'CLINICAL_ARTIFACT',resourceId:transferId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    const tr:any=await this.tables.findById('artifact_transfer_attempts',transferId,client,true);
    const changes:any={transfer_status:d.status};
    let eventType=d.status;
    if(d.status==='IN_PROGRESS' && tr.transfer_status==='PAUSED') eventType='RESUMED';
    if(d.status==='PAUSED') changes.last_progress_at=new Date();
    if(d.status==='COMPLETED'){
      changes.received_bytes=d.receivedBytes??tr.received_bytes; changes.observed_sha256=d.observedSha256; changes.completed_at=new Date();
      if(tr.expected_bytes!==null && Number(changes.received_bytes)!==Number(tr.expected_bytes)) throw new ConflictException('Completed transfer byte count mismatch');
    }
    if(d.status==='FAILED'){ if(!d.failureCode) throw new ConflictException('failureCode is required'); changes.failed_at=new Date(); changes.failure_code=d.failureCode; }
    const updated=await this.tables.updateById('artifact_transfer_attempts',transferId,changes,client);
    await this.tables.insert('artifact_transfer_events',{
      transfer_attempt_id:transferId,event_type:eventType,bytes_received:changes.received_bytes??tr.received_bytes,
      event_code:d.failureCode,request_id:ctx.requestId,
    },client);
    return updated;
   }});
 }

 acceptRepeat(repeatId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
   return this.transitionRepeat(repeatId,'ACCEPTED',ctx,{
     reasonCode:d.reasonCode,newAcquisitionId:d.newAcquisitionId,capabilityGrantId:d.acceptanceCapabilityGrantId
   });
 }
 satisfyRepeat(repeatId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
   return this.transitionRepeat(repeatId,'SATISFIED',ctx,{reasonCode:d.reasonCode});
 }
 declineRepeat(repeatId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
   return this.transitionRepeat(repeatId,'DECLINED',ctx,{reasonCode:d.reasonCode,declineReasonCode:d.declineReasonCode??d.reasonCode});
 }
 cancelRepeat(repeatId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
   return this.transitionRepeat(repeatId,'CANCELLED',ctx,{reasonCode:d.reasonCode});
 }

 completeExamOrder(orderId:string,d:any,ctx:ClinicalRequestContext){
  return this.commands.execute({context:ctx,permission:'CLINICAL_EXAM_ORDER',action:'EXAM_ORDER_COMPLETE_ACQUISITION',resourceType:'EXAM_ORDER',resourceId:orderId,
   handler:async(client)=>{
    await this.tables.call('complete_exam_order_from_acquisition',[
      orderId,d.acquisitionSessionId,d.expectedOrderVersion,ctx.userId,ctx.workforceId??null,ctx.requestId
    ],client);
    return this.tables.findById('exam_orders',orderId,client);
   }});
 }
